\"content\": \"Hi\"}],\n        \"thinking\": {\"type\": \"enabled\", \"budget_tokens\": 10000},\n        \"metadata\": {\"user_id\": \"123\"},\n        \"stream\": True,\n    }\n    result = anthropic_to_tensorzero_request(anthropic_body, \"claude_sonnet\")\n    # thinking and metadata should not appear in params\n    assert \"thinking\" not in result.get(\"params\", {}).get(\"chat_completion\", {})\n    assert \"metadata\" not in result.get(\"params\", {}).get(\"chat_completion\", {})\n\n\n# ── Response streaming translation ──\n\n\ndef test_text_chunk_to_anthropic_events():\n    state = StreamState()\n    chunk = {\n        \"inference_id\": \"inf_123\",\n        \"episode_id\": \"ep_456\",\n        \"variant_name\": \"default\",\n        \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"id\": \"0\", \"text\": \"Hello\"}],\n    }\n    events = tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk, state)\n    # Should produce: content_block_start (new block) + content_block_delta\n    event_types = [e[\"type\"] for e in events]\n    assert \"content_block_start\" in event_types\n    assert \"content_block_delta\" in event_types\n\n\ndef test_text_chunk_continuation():\n    state = StreamState()\n    # First chunk starts the block\n    chunk1 = {\n        \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"id\": \"0\", \"text\": \"Hello\"}],\n    }\n    tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk1, state)\n    # Second chunk continues\n    chunk2 = {\n        \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"id\": \"0\", \"text\": \" world\"}],\n    }\n    events = tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk2, state)\n    # Should produce only a delta, not another start\n    event_types = [e[\"type\"] for e in events]\n    assert \"content_block_start\" not in event_types\n    assert \"content_block_delta\" in event_types\n    delta_event = [e for e in events if e[\"type\"] == \"content_block_delta\"][0]\n    assert delta_event[\"delta\"][\"text\"] == \" world\"\n\n\ndef test_tool_call_chunk_to_anthropic_events():\n    state = StreamState()\n    chunk = {\n        \"content\": [\n            {\n                \"type\": \"tool_call\",\n                \"id\": \"tool_abc\",\n                \"raw_name\": \"get_weather\",\n                \"raw_arguments\": '{\"location\": \"SF\"}',\n            }\n        ],\n    }\n    events = tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk, state)\n    event_types = [e[\"type\"] for e in events]\n    assert \"content_block_start\" in event_types\n    start = [e for e in events if e[\"type\"] == \"content_block_start\"][0]\n    assert start[\"content_block\"][\"type\"] == \"tool_use\"\n    assert start[\"content_block\"][\"name\"] == \"get_weather\"\n\n\ndef test_done_produces_message_delta_and_stop():\n    state = StreamState()\n    # Start a text block first\n    chunk = {\"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"id\": \"0\", \"text\": \"Hi\"}]}\n    tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk, state)\n    # Now finalize\n    events = state.finalize(usage={\"input_tokens\": 10, \"output_tokens\": 5})\n    event_types = [e[\"type\"] for e in events]\n    assert \"content_block_stop\" in event_types\n    assert \"message_delta\" in event_types\n    assert \"message_stop\" in event_types\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_llm_proxy_translate.py -v`\nExpected: FAIL — `ModuleNotFoundError`\n\n**Step 3: Create `backend/app/llm_proxy_translate.py`**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"Translate between Anthropic Messages API and TensorZero /inference formats.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\n\ndef anthropic_to_tensorzero_request(\n    anthropic_body: dict, tensorzero_model: str\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Convert an Anthropic Messages API request body to TensorZero /inference format.\"\"\"\n    messages = []\n\n    # System prompt → system message\n    system = anthropic_body.get(\"system\")\n    if system:\n        if isinstance(system, str):\n            messages.append({\n                \"role\": \"system\",\n                \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"value\": system}],\n            })\n        elif isinstance(system, list):\n            # Anthropic also accepts system as list of content blocks\n            content = []\n            for block in system:\n                if isinstance(block, dict) and block.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n                    content.append({\"type\": \"text\", \"value\": block.get(\"text\", \"\")})\n            if content:\n                messages.append({\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": content})\n\n    # Convert messages\n    for msg in anthropic_body.get(\"messages\", []):\n        role = msg.get(\"role\", \"user\")\n        content = msg.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if isinstance(content, str):\n            messages.append({\n                \"role\": role,\n                \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"value\": content}],\n            })\n        elif isinstance(content, list):\n            tz_content = []\n            for block in content:\n                if isinstance(block, dict):\n                    block_type = block.get(\"type\", \"text\")\n                    if block_type == \"text\":\n                        tz_content.append({\n                            \"type\": \"text\",\n                            \"value\": block.get(\"text\", \"\"),\n                        })\n                    elif block_type == \"tool_use\":\n                        tz_content.append({\n                            \"type\": \"tool_call\",\n                            \"id\": block.get(\"id\", \"\"),\n                            \"name\": block.get(\"name\", \"\"),\n                            \"arguments\": json.dumps(block.get(\"input\", {})),\n                        })\n                    elif block_type == \"tool_result\":\n                        result_content = block.get(\"content\", \"\")\n                        if isinstance(result_content, list):\n                            text_parts = []\n                            for part in result_content:\n                                if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n                                    text_parts.append(part.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n                            result_text = \"\\n\".join(text_parts)\n                        elif isinstance(result_content, str):\n                            result_text = result_content\n                        else:\n                            result_text = str(result_content)\n                        tz_content.append({\n                            \"type\": \"tool_result\",\n                            \"id\": block.get(\"tool_use_id\", \"\"),\n                            \"value\": result_text,\n                        })\n                    # Skip Anthropic-specific types like \"thinking\", \"image\"\n            if tz_content:\n                messages.append({\"role\": role, \"content\": tz_content})\n\n    # Build params\n    params: dict = {}\n    chat_params: dict = {}\n\n    max_tokens = anthropic_body.get(\"max_tokens\")\n    if max_tokens:\n        chat_params[\"max_tokens\"] = max_tokens\n\n    temperature = anthropic_body.get(\"temperature\")\n    if temperature is not None:\n        chat_params[\"temperature\"] = temperature\n\n    top_p = anthropic_body.get(\"top_p\")\n    if top_p is not None:\n        chat_params[\"top_p\"] = top_p\n\n    tools = anthropic_body.get(\"tools\")\n    if tools:\n        chat_params[\"tools\"] = tools\n\n    tool_choice = anthropic_body.get(\"tool_choice\")\n    if tool_choice:\n        chat_params[\"tool_choice\"] = tool_choice\n\n    if chat_params:\n        params[\"chat_completion\"] = chat_params\n\n    result: dict = {\n        \"function_name\": \"chat\",\n        \"model_name\": tensorzero_model,\n        \"input\": {\"messages\": messages},\n        \"stream\": anthropic_body.get(\"stream\", False),\n    }\n    if params:\n        result[\"params\"] = params\n\n    return result\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass StreamState:\n    \"\"\"Track state during streaming response translation.\"\"\"\n\n    message_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f\"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]}\")\n    model: str = \"unknown\"\n    # Track which content block IDs have been started\n    started_blocks: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    # Map block ID → anthropic index\n    next_index: int = 0\n    # Track the current open block\n    current_block_id: str | None = None\n    message_started: bool = False\n\n    def get_or_create_index(self, block_id: str) -> tuple[int, bool]:\n        \"\"\"Get the Anthropic index for a block ID, creating if new.\n        Returns (index, is_new).\"\"\"\n        if block_id in self.started_blocks:\n            return self.started_blocks[block_id], False\n        idx = self.next_index\n        self.started_blocks[block_id] = idx\n        self.next_index += 1\n        return idx, True\n\n    def finalize(\n        self,\n        usage: dict | None = None,\n        stop_reason: str = \"end_turn\",\n    ) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Generate closing events for the stream.\"\"\"\n        events = []\n        # Close any open block\n        if self.current_block_id is not None:\n            idx = self.started_blocks.get(self.current_block_id, 0)\n            events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": idx})\n            self.current_block_id = None\n\n        # message_delta\n        delta_event: dict = {\n            \"type\": \"message_delta\",\n            \"delta\": {\"stop_reason\": stop_reason, \"stop_sequence\": None},\n        }\n        if usage:\n            delta_event[\"usage\"] = usage\n        events.append(delta_event)\n\n        # message_stop\n        events.append({\"type\": \"message_stop\"})\n        return events\n\n\ndef tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(\n    chunk: dict, state: StreamState\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Convert a TensorZero streaming chunk to Anthropic SSE event dicts.\n\n    Returns a list of event dicts, each with a \"type\" key matching the\n    Anthropic SSE event name.\n    \"\"\"\n    events: list[dict] = []\n\n    # Emit message_start on first chunk\n    if not state.message_started:\n        state.message_started = True\n        model = chunk.get(\"variant_name\", state.model)\n        state.model = model\n        events.append({\n            \"type\": \"message_start\",\n            \"message\": {\n                \"id\": state.message_id,\n                \"type\": \"message\",\n                \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                \"content\": [],\n                \"model\": model,\n                \"stop_reason\": None,\n                \"stop_sequence\": None,\n                \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\": 0, \"output_tokens\": 0},\n            },\n        })\n\n    content_blocks = chunk.get(\"content\", [])\n    for block in content_blocks:\n        block_type = block.get(\"type\")\n        block_id = block.get(\"id\", \"0\")\n\n        if block_type == \"text\":\n            idx, is_new = state.get_or_create_index(block_id)\n            if is_new:\n                # Close previous block if different\n                if state.current_block_id is not None and state.current_block_id != block_id:\n                    prev_idx = state.started_blocks.get(state.current_block_id, 0)\n                    events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": prev_idx})\n                state.current_block_id = block_id\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"content_block\": {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"\"},\n                })\n            text = block.get(\"text\", \"\")\n            if text:\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"delta\": {\"type\": \"text_delta\", \"text\": text},\n                })\n\n        elif block_type == \"tool_call\":\n            tool_id = block.get(\"id\", f\"toolu_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]}\")\n            idx, is_new = state.get_or_create_index(tool_id)\n            if is_new:\n                if state.current_block_id is not None and state.current_block_id != tool_id:\n                    prev_idx = state.started_blocks.get(state.current_block_id, 0)\n                    events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": prev_idx})\n                state.current_block_id = tool_id\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"content_block\": {\n                        \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                        \"id\": tool_id,\n                        \"name\": block.get(\"raw_name\", \"\"),\n                        \"input\": {},\n                    },\n                })\n            raw_args = block.get(\"raw_arguments\", \"\")\n            if raw_args:\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"delta\": {\n                        \"type\": \"input_json_delta\",\n                        \"partial_json\": raw_args,\n                    },\n                })\n\n        elif block_type == \"thought\":\n            idx, is_new = state.get_or_create_index(f\"thinking_{block_id}\")\n            if is_new:\n                if state.current_block_id is not None:\n                    prev_idx = state.started_blocks.get(state.current_block_id, 0)\n                    events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": prev_idx})\n                state.current_block_id = f\"thinking_{block_id}\"\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"content_block\": {\"type\": \"thinking\", \"thinking\": \"\"},\n                })\n            text = block.get(\"text\", \"\")\n            if text:\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"delta\": {\"type\": \"thinking_delta\", \"thinking\": text},\n                })\n\n    # Capture usage if present\n    usage = chunk.get(\"usage\")\n    if usage:\n        state.model = chunk.get(\"variant_name\", state.model)\n\n    return events\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_llm_proxy_translate.py -v`\nExpected: all tests PASS\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add backend/app/llm_proxy_translate.py backend/tests/test_llm_proxy_translate.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(llm-proxy): add Anthropic ↔ TensorZero request/response translator\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 7: Create the LLM proxy router\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `backend/app/llm_proxy.py`\n- Create: `backend/tests/test_llm_proxy.py`\n\nThis is the FastAPI router that replaces `proxy.py`. It handles UUID token validation and forwards requests through TensorZero.\n\n**Step 1: Write the failing tests**\n\nCreate `backend/tests/test_llm_proxy.py`:\n\n```python\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.llm_proxy import router\nfrom app.token_store import proxy_token_store\n\n\ndef make_app():\n    app = FastAPI()\n    app.include_router(router)\n    return app\n\n\ndef test_rejects_missing_auth():\n    client = TestClient(make_app(), raise_server_exceptions=False)\n    response = client.post(\"/llm-proxy/v1/messages\", json={})\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n\n\ndef test_rejects_invalid_x_api_key():\n    client = TestClient(make_app(), raise_server_exceptions=False)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/llm-proxy/v1/messages\",\n        json={},\n        headers={\"x-api-key\": \"not-a-valid-uuid\"},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n\n\ndef test_rejects_invalid_bearer_token():\n    client = TestClient(make_app(), raise_server_exceptions=False)\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/llm-proxy/v1/messages\",\n        json={},\n        headers={\"Authorization\": \"Bearer not-a-valid-uuid\"},\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n\n\ndef test_rejects_revoked_token():\n    token=[REDACTED].create_token()\n    try:\n        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(token)\n        client = TestClient(make_app(), raise_server_exceptions=False)\n        response = client.post(\n            \"/llm-proxy/v1/messages\",\n            json={},\n            headers={\"x-api-key\": token},\n        )\n        assert response.status_code == 401\n    finally:\n        proxy_token_store.revoke_token(token)\n\n\ndef test_models_endpoint_no_auth_required():\n    \"\"\"GET /llm-proxy/v1/models should not require auth (SDK discovery).\"\"\"\n    client = TestClient(make_app(), raise_server_exceptions=False)\n    response = client.get(\"/llm-proxy/v1/models\")\n    # Should return 200 with a models list, not 401\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    data = response.json()\n    assert \"data\" in data\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_llm_proxy.py -v`\nExpected: FAIL — `ModuleNotFoundError`\n\n**Step 3: Create `backend/app/llm_proxy.py`**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"LLM proxy adapter: Anthropic Messages API → TensorZero gateway.\n\nReplaces proxy.py. Validates session UUID tokens, translates requests\nto TensorZero /inference format, and translates streaming responses\nback to Anthropic SSE format.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse, JSONResponse\nimport httpx\n\nfrom app.config import TENSORZERO_BASE_URL\nfrom app.token_store import proxy_token_store\nfrom app.model_map import resolve_model\nfrom app.llm_proxy_translate import (\n    anthropic_to_tensorzero_request,\n    tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events,\n    StreamState,\n)\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/llm-proxy\")\n\n# Read-only discovery endpoints (no auth required)\n_UNAUTHENTICATED_PATHS = {\"v1/models\"}\n\n\ndef _extract_session_token(request: Request) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract session token from x-api-key header or Authorization: Bearer.\"\"\"\n    return (\n        request.headers.get(\"x-api-key\")\n        or request.headers.get(\"authorization\", \"\")[len(\"Bearer \"):]\n        or None\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/v1/models\")\nasync def list_models():\n    \"\"\"Return available models for SDK discovery.\n\n    The Claude Agent SDK calls GET /v1/models during startup to resolve\n    short model aliases. We return a static list based on the model map.\n    \"\"\"\n    from app.model_map import _DEFAULT_MAP\n\n    models = []\n    seen = set()\n    for sdk_name in _DEFAULT_MAP:\n        if sdk_name not in seen:\n            models.append({\n                \"id\": sdk_name,\n                \"object\": \"model\",\n                \"created\": 0,\n                \"owned_by\": \"tensorzero\",\n            })\n            seen.add(sdk_name)\n\n    return {\"object\": \"list\", \"data\": models}\n\n\n@router.post(\"/v1/messages\")\nasync def proxy_messages(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Handle POST /v1/messages — the main Anthropic Messages API endpoint.\"\"\"\n    session_token=[REDACTED](request)\n    if not session_token or not proxy_token_store.validate_token(session_token):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\"Invalid or expired session token\")\n\n    body = await request.json()\n    model_name = body.get(\"model\", \"claude-sonnet-4-6\")\n    tensorzero_model = resolve_model(model_name)\n    is_streaming = body.get(\"stream\", False)\n\n    tz_request = anthropic_to_tensorzero_request(body, tensorzero_model)\n\n    if is_streaming:\n        return StreamingResponse(\n            _stream_response(tz_request, model_name),\n            media_type=\"text/event-stream\",\n            headers={\n                \"Cache-Control\": \"no-cache\",\n                \"Connection\": \"keep-alive\",\n                \"X-Accel-Buffering\": \"no\",\n            },\n        )\n    else:\n        return await _non_streaming_response(tz_request, model_name)\n\n\nasync def _stream_response(tz_request: dict, model_name: str):\n    \"\"\"Stream TensorZero response as Anthropic-format SSE events.\"\"\"\n    state = StreamState(model=model_name)\n\n    client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0))\n    try:\n        async with client.stream(\n            \"POST\",\n            f\"{TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}/inference\",\n            json=tz_request,\n        ) as response:\n            if response.status_code != 200:\n                error_body = await response.aread()\n                error_text = error_body.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n                logger.error(\n                    \"TensorZero returned %d: %s\", response.status_code, error_text\n                )\n                # Map to Anthropic error format\n                yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'error': {'type': 'api_error', 'message': error_text}})}\\n\\n\"\n                return\n\n            async for line in response.aiter_lines():\n                if not line.startswith(\"data: \"):\n                    continue\n                raw = line[6:].strip()\n                if raw == \"[DONE]\":\n                    break\n                try:\n                    chunk = json.loads(raw)\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                    continue\n\n                events = tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk, state)\n                for event in events:\n                    event_name = event[\"type\"]\n                    yield f\"event: {event_name}\\ndata: {json.dumps(event)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        # Finalize — close any open blocks and emit message_delta + message_stop\n        usage = {\"output_tokens\": 0}\n        final_events = state.finalize(usage=usage)\n        for event in final_events:\n            event_name = event[\"type\"]\n            yield f\"event: {event_name}\\ndata: {json.dumps(event)}\\n\\n\"\n\n    except httpx.ConnectError:\n        logger.error(\"Cannot connect to TensorZero at %s\", TENSORZERO_BASE_URL)\n        yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'error': {'type': 'api_error', 'message': f'LLM gateway unreachable at {TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}'}})}\\n\\n\"\n    except Exception:\n        logger.exception(\"Error streaming from TensorZero\")\n        yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'error': {'type': 'api_error', 'message': 'Internal proxy error'}})}\\n\\n\"\n    finally:\n        await client.aclose()\n\n\nasync def _non_streaming_response(tz_request: dict, model_name: str) -> JSONResponse:\n    \"\"\"Handle non-streaming inference and return Anthropic-format JSON.\"\"\"\n    tz_request[\"stream\"] = False\n\n    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0)) as client:\n        try:\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}/inference\",\n                json=tz_request,\n            )\n        except httpx.ConnectError:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=502,\n                detail=f\"LLM gateway unreachable at {TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}\",\n            )\n\n        if resp.status_code != 200:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=resp.status_code, detail=resp.text)\n\n        tz_data = resp.json()\n\n    # Convert TensorZero response to Anthropic format\n    content = []\n    for block in tz_data.get(\"content\", []):\n        if block.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n            content.append({\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": block.get(\"text\", \"\")})\n        elif block.get(\"type\") == \"tool_call\":\n            arguments = block.get(\"raw_arguments\", \"{}\")\n            try:\n                parsed_args = json.loads(arguments)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                parsed_args = {}\n            content.append({\n                \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                \"id\": block.get(\"id\", \"\"),\n                \"name\": block.get(\"raw_name\", block.get(\"name\", \"\")),\n                \"input\": parsed_args,\n            })\n\n    usage = tz_data.get(\"usage\", {})\n\n    return JSONResponse({\n        \"id\": tz_data.get(\"inference_id\", \"\"),\n        \"type\": \"message\",\n        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n        \"content\": content,\n        \"model\": model_name,\n        \"stop_reason\": \"end_turn\",\n        \"stop_sequence\": None,\n        \"usage\": {\n            \"input_tokens\": usage.get(\"input_tokens\", 0),\n            \"output_tokens\": usage.get(\"output_tokens\", 0),\n        },\n    })\n\n\n@router.api_route(\"/{path:path}\", methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\", \"PATCH\"])\nasync def catch_all(path: str, request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Catch-all for unhandled paths — return 404.\"\"\"\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\"Unknown path: /llm-proxy/{path}\")\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_llm_proxy.py -v`\nExpected: all 5 tests PASS\n\n**Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add backend/app/llm_proxy.py backend/tests/test_llm_proxy.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(llm-proxy): add LLM proxy router with auth and TensorZero forwarding\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 8: Wire up the new LLM proxy in main.py and agent.py\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`\n- Modify: `backend/app/agent.py`\n\n**Step 1: Update `backend/app/main.py`**\n\nReplace `proxy_module` import and usage with `llm_proxy` and `token_store`:\n\nAt line 12, change:\n```python\nfrom app import      7→```
     8→Task tool (general-purpose):
     9→  description: "Review spec compliance for Task N"
    10→  prompt: |
    11→    You are reviewing whether an implementation matches its specification.
    12→
    13→    ## What Was Requested
    14→
    15→    [FULL TEXT of task requirements]
    16→
    17→    ## What Implementer Claims They Built
    18→
    19→    [From implementer's report]
    20→
    21→    ## CRITICAL: Do Not Trust the Report
    22→
    23→    The implementer finished suspiciously quickly. Their report may be incomplete,
    24→    inaccurate, or optimistic. You MUST verify everything independently.
    25→
    26→    **DO NOT:**
    27→    - Take their word for what they implemented
    28→    - Trust their claims about completeness
    29→    - Accept their interpretation of requirements
    30→
    31→    **DO:**
    32→    - Read the actual code they wrote
    33→    - Compare actual implementation to requirements line by line
    34→    - Check for missing pieces they claimed to implement
    35→    - Look for extra features they didn't mention
    36→
    37→    ## Your Job
    38→
    39→    Read the implementation code and verify:
    40→
    41→    **Missing requirements:**
    42→    - Did they implement everything that was requested?
    43→    - Are there requirements they skipped or missed?
    44→    - Did they claim something works but didn't actually implement it?
    45→
    46→    **Extra/unneeded work:**
    47→    - Did they build things that weren't requested?
    48→    - Did they over-engineer or add unnecessary features?
    49→    - Did they add "nice to haves" that weren't in spec?
    50→
    51→    **Misunderstandings:**
    52→    - Did they interpret requirements differently than intended?
    53→    - Did they solve the wrong problem?
    54→    - Did they implement the right feature but wrong way?
    55→
    56→    **Verify by reading code, not by trusting report.**
    57→
    58→    Report:
    59→    - ✅ Spec compliant (if everything matches after code inspection)

## Report

### What I implemented

Updated `docker-compose.yml` to add the TensorZero gateway as a new service and wire it into the existing `app` service. Specifically:

1. **Added `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL: http://tensorzero:3000`** to the `app` service's `environment` block so the backend knows where to reach the gateway.
2. **Added `depends_on: - tensorzero`** to the `app` service so Docker Compose ensures the gateway starts before the application.
3. **Added the `tensorzero` service** between `app` and `sidecar` with:
   - Image: `tensorzero/gateway:latest`
   - Container name: `tensorzero-gateway`
   - Volume mount for the config file: `./tensorzero.toml:/etc/tensorzero/tensorzero.toml:ro`
   - Environment variables for API key passthrough (`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `GOOGLE_API_KEY`, `MISTRAL_API_KEY`)
   - Connected to the `agent-sandbox` network
   - Restart policy: `unless-stopped`
4. **Removed the explanatory comment** from the `sidecar` service to match the spec.

## Report

### What was implemented

Updated the backend configuration to support TensorZero as the LLM gateway:

1. **Made `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` optional** -- Removed the `if not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` warning block (which imported `warnings` and raised a `RuntimeWarning`). Replaced it with comments explaining the key is optional since primary API keys now live in TensorZero.

2. **Added `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL` config variable** -- Defaults to `http://localhost:3000`, which is the TensorZero gateway port defined in the Docker Compose service from Task 2.

3. **Updated `.env.example`** -- Added a comment clarifying that `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is optional in the backend, and added a new `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL` entry under a "TensorZero LLM gateway" section.

### Files changed

- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/app/config.py` -- Removed 7-line warning block, added 2-line comment and `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL` variable.
- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/.env.example` -- Added comment for `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and new `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL` entry.

### Issues or concerns

None. The changes are straightforward configuration additions. The `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL` default of `http://localhost:3000` matches the port exposed by the TensorZero service in Docker Compose (configured in Task 2).

{
  "description": "Implement Task 4: Create token store",
  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 4: Create the token store module\n\n## Task Description\n\nExtract the ProxyTokenStore class from `backend/app/proxy.py` into its own module `backend/app/token_store.py`, and create tests.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `backend/app/token_store.py`\n- Create: `backend/tests/test_token_store.py`\n\n### Step 1: Write the failing tests\n\nCreate `backend/tests/test_token_store.py`:\n\n```python\nimport uuid as uuid_module\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom app.token_store import ProxyTokenStore\n\n\ndef test_create_token_returns_uuid_string():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    token = store.create_token()\n    parsed = uuid_module.UUID(token)\n    assert parsed.version == 4\n\n\ndef test_valid_token_passes_validation():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    token = store.create_token()\n    assert store.validate_token(token) is True\n\n\ndef test_unknown_token_fails_validation():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    assert store.validate_token(\"not-a-real-token\") is False\n\n\ndef test_revoked_token_fails_validation():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    token = store.create_token()\n    store.revoke_token(token)\n    assert store.validate_token(token) is False\n\n\ndef test_expired_token_fails_validation():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    token = store.create_token()\n    store._tokens[token] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)\n    assert store.validate_token(token) is False\n\n\ndef test_revoke_nonexistent_token_is_safe():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    store.revoke_token(\"ghost-token\")  # must not raise\n\n\ndef test_cleanup_expired_removes_stale_tokens():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    live = store.create_token()\n    stale = store.create_token()\n    store._tokens[stale] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)\n    removed = store.cleanup_expired()\n    assert removed == 1\n    assert store.validate_token(live) is True\n    assert store.validate_token(stale) is False\n\n\ndef test_cleanup_expired_on_empty_store_returns_zero():\n    store = ProxyTokenStore()\n    assert store.cleanup_expired() == 0\n```\n\n### Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_token_store.py -v`\nExpected: FAIL — `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'app.token_store'`\n\n### Step 3: Create `backend/app/token_store.py`\n\n```python\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\n\nclass ProxyTokenStore:\n    \"\"\"Session-scoped UUID token store for credential proxy authentication.\n\n    Each agent session receives a short-lived UUID token. The sidecar container\n    uses this token in place of real API keys. Tokens are multi-use (one per\n    session) and explicitly revoked when the session ends.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int = 600):\n        self._ttl = ttl_seconds\n        self._tokens: dict[str, datetime] = {}\n\n    def create_token(self) -> str:\n        token = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        self._tokens[token] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=self._ttl)\n        return token\n\n    def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:\n        expiry = self._tokens.get(token)\n        if expiry is None:\n            return False\n        if datetime.now(timezone.utc) > expiry:\n            self._tokens.pop(token, None)\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def revoke_token(self, token: str) -> None:\n        self._tokens.pop(token, None)\n\n    def cleanup_expired(self) -> int:\n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        expired = [t for t, exp in self._tokens.items() if now > exp]\n        for t in expired:\n            del self._tokens[t]\n        return len(expired)\n\n\nproxy_token_store = ProxyTokenStore()\n```\n\n### Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_token_store.py -v`\nExpected: all 8 tests PASS\n\n### Step 5: Commit\n\n```bash\ngit add backend/app/token_store.py backend/tests/test_token_store.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(llm-proxy): extract ProxyTokenStore into token_store module\"\n```\n\n## Context\n\nThis is task 4 of 10. The ProxyTokenStore class currently lives in `proxy.py`. We're extracting it so the new `llm_proxy.py` can import it. The old `proxy.py` will be removed in Task 9.\n\n## Your Job\n\n1. ## Report

### What I Implemented

Extracted the `ProxyTokenStore` class from `backend/app/proxy.py` into a standalone module `backend/app/token_store.py`. The new module contains:

- **`ProxyTokenStore` class** -- A session-scoped UUID token store for credential proxy authentication. It manages short-lived UUID tokens with configurable TTL (default 600 seconds), supporting creation, validation, revocation, and cleanup of expired tokens.
- **`proxy_token_store` module-level singleton** -- A pre-instantiated default instance for use by other modules.

### What I Tested and Test Results

Created `backend/tests/test_token_store.py` with 8 test cases:

| Test | Description | Result |
|------|-------------|--------|
| `test_create_token_returns_uuid_string` | Token is a valid UUID v4 string | PASSED |
| `test_valid_token_passes_validation` | Freshly created token validates successfully | PASSED |
| `test_unknown_token_fails_validation` | Unknown token string is rejected | PASSED |
| `test_revoked_token_fails_validation` | Revoked token no longer validates | PASSED |
| `test_expired_token_fails_validation` | Token past its expiry is rejected and cleaned up | PASSED |
| `test_revoke_nonexistent_token_is_safe` | Revoking a non-existent token does not raise | PASSED |
| `test_cleanup_expired_removes_stale_tokens` | `cleanup_expired()` removes stale tokens, keeps live ones, returns count | PASSED |
| `test_cleanup_expired_on_empty_store_returns_zero` | `cleanup_expired()` on empty store returns 0 | PASSED |

TDD was followed: tests were written first and confirmed to fail with `ModuleNotFoundError`, then the implementation was written, and all 8 tests passed.

### Files Changed

- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/app/token_store.py` (44 lines)
- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/tests/test_token_store.py` (55 lines)

### Self-Review Findings

1. The implementation in `token_store.py` preserves the exact same logic and docstring commentary from `proxy.py`, ensuring behavioral equivalence. The important multi-use comment from `validate_token` in `proxy.py` was incorporated into the class-level docstring rather than an inline comment, since the new module serves as the canonical reference.

2. The existing `proxy.py` still contains its own copy of `ProxyTokenStore`. This duplication is intentional per the plan -- `proxy.py` will be updated to import from `token_store.py` (or removed entirely) in Task 9.

3. No existing tests were broken by this change. The pre-existing test failures in the suite are all from missing dependencies (`claude_agent_sdk`, `docker`, `mcp`) or sandbox file access restrictions, none related to these changes.

### Issues or Concerns

None. The extraction is clean and self-contained. The module is ready for import by the new `llm_proxy.py` router in Task 7.

---

## Report

### What I implemented

Created `backend/app/model_map.py` -- a module that maps SDK model names to TensorZero model identifiers. It contains:

- `_DEFAULT_MAP`: A dictionary mapping model names from various providers (Anthropic full names like `"claude-sonnet-4-6"`, short aliases like `"haiku"`, OpenAI models like `"gpt-4o"`, Google models like `"gemini-2.0-flash"`, and Mistral models like `"mistral-large-latest"`) to their corresponding TensorZero model identifiers (e.g., `"claude_sonnet"`, `"gpt_4o"`, `"gemini_flash"`).
- `resolve_model(model_name: str) -> str`: A function that looks up the model name in the mapping and returns the TensorZero identifier, or returns the input unchanged for unknown/custom models.

### What I tested and test results

Created `backend/tests/test_model_map.py` with 7 tests:

1. `test_resolve_known_anthropic_model` -- full Anthropic name `"claude-sonnet-4-6"` maps to `"claude_sonnet"`
2. `test_resolve_short_alias_haiku` -- short alias `"haiku"` maps to `"claude_haiku"`
3. `test_resolve_short_alias_sonnet` -- short alias `"sonnet"` maps to `"claude_sonnet"`
4. `test_resolve_openai_model` -- OpenAI model `"gpt-4o"` maps to `"gpt_4o"`
5. `test_resolve_unknown_model_returns_as_is` -- unknown model `"my-custom-model"` passes through unchanged
6. `test_resolve_claude_haiku_full_name` -- full name `"claude-haiku-4-5"` maps to `"claude_haiku"`
7. `test_resolve_claude_opus_full_name` -- full name `"claude-opus-4-6"` maps to `"claude_opus"`

TDD process:
- Tests initially failed with `ModuleNotFoundError` (as expected)
- After implementation, all 7 tests passed in 0.01s

### Files changed

- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/app/model_map.py`
- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/tests/test_model_map.py`

### Issues or concerns

None. The implementation is straightforward and all tests pass.

isinstance(system, str):\n            messages.append({\n                \"role\": \"system\",\n                \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"value\": system}],\n            })\n        elif isinstance(system, list):\n            content = []\n            for block in system:\n                if isinstance(block, dict) and block.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n                    content.append({\"type\": \"text\", \"value\": block.get(\"text\", \"\")})\n            if content:\n                messages.append({\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": content})\n\n    # Convert messages\n    for msg in anthropic_body.get(\"messages\", []):\n        role = msg.get(\"role\", \"user\")\n        content = msg.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if isinstance(content, str):\n            messages.append({\n                \"role\": role,\n                \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"value\": content}],\n            })\n        elif isinstance(content, list):\n            tz_content = []\n            for block in content:\n                if isinstance(block, dict):\n                    block_type = block.get(\"type\", \"text\")\n                    if block_type == \"text\":\n                        tz_content.append({\n                            \"type\": \"text\",\n                            \"value\": block.get(\"text\", \"\"),\n                        })\n                    elif block_type == \"tool_use\":\n                        tz_content.append({\n                            \"type\": \"tool_call\",\n                            \"id\": block.get(\"id\", \"\"),\n                            \"name\": block.get(\"name\", \"\"),\n                            \"arguments\": json.dumps(block.get(\"input\", {})),\n                        })\n                    elif block_type == \"tool_result\":\n                        result_content = block.get(\"content\", \"\")\n                        if isinstance(result_content, list):\n                            text_parts = []\n                            for part in result_content:\n                                if isinstance(part, dict) and part.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n                                    text_parts.append(part.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n                            result_text = \"\\n\".join(text_parts)\n                        elif isinstance(result_content, str):\n                            result_text = result_content\n                        else:\n                            result_text = str(result_content)\n                        tz_content.append({\n                            \"type\": \"tool_result\",\n                            \"id\": block.get(\"tool_use_id\", \"\"),\n                            \"value\": result_text,\n                        })\n            if tz_content:\n                messages.append({\"role\": role, \"content\": tz_content})\n\n    # Build params\n    params: dict = {}\n    chat_params: dict = {}\n\n    max_tokens = anthropic_body.get(\"max_tokens\")\n    if max_tokens:\n        chat_params[\"max_tokens\"] = max_tokens\n\n    temperature = anthropic_body.get(\"temperature\")\n    if temperature is not None:\n        chat_params[\"temperature\"] = temperature\n\n    top_p = anthropic_body.get(\"top_p\")\n    if top_p is not None:\n        chat_params[\"top_p\"] = top_p\n\n    tools = anthropic_body.get(\"tools\")\n    if tools:\n        chat_params[\"tools\"] = tools\n\n    tool_choice = anthropic_body.get(\"tool_choice\")\n    if tool_choice:\n        chat_params[\"tool_choice\"] = tool_choice\n\n    if chat_params:\n        params[\"chat_completion\"] = chat_params\n\n    result: dict = {\n        \"function_name\": \"chat\",\n        \"model_name\": tensorzero_model,\n        \"input\": {\"messages\": messages},\n        \"stream\": anthropic_body.get(\"stream\", False),\n    }\n    if params:\n        result[\"params\"] = params\n\n    return result\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass StreamState:\n    \"\"\"Track state during streaming response translation.\"\"\"\n\n    message_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f\"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]}\")\n    model: str = \"unknown\"\n    started_blocks: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    next_index: int = 0\n    current_block_id: str | None = None\n    message_started: bool = False\n\n    def get_or_create_index(self, block_id: str) -> tuple[int, bool]:\n        \"\"\"Get the Anthropic index for a block ID, creating if new.\"\"\"\n        if block_id in self.started_blocks:\n            return self.started_blocks[block_id], False\n        idx = self.next_index\n        self.started_blocks[block_id] = idx\n        self.next_index += 1\n        return idx, True\n\n    def finalize(\n        self,\n        usage: dict | None = None,\n        stop_reason: str = \"end_turn\",\n    ) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Generate closing events for the stream.\"\"\"\n        events = []\n        if self.current_block_id is not None:\n            idx = self.started_blocks.get(self.current_block_id, 0)\n            events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": idx})\n            self.current_block_id = None\n\n        delta_event: dict = {\n            \"type\": \"message_delta\",\n            \"delta\": {\"stop_reason\": stop_reason, \"stop_sequence\": None},\n        }\n        if usage:\n            delta_event[\"usage\"] = usage\n        events.append(delta_event)\n\n        events.append({\"type\": \"message_stop\"})\n        return events\n\n\ndef tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(\n    chunk: dict, state: StreamState\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Convert a TensorZero streaming chunk to Anthropic SSE event dicts.\"\"\"\n    events: list[dict] = []\n\n    if not state.message_started:\n        state.message_started = True\n        model = chunk.get(\"variant_name\", state.model)\n        state.model = model\n        events.append({\n            \"type\": \"message_start\",\n            \"message\": {\n                \"id\": state.message_id,\n                \"type\": \"message\",\n                \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                \"content\": [],\n                \"model\": model,\n                \"stop_reason\": None,\n                \"stop_sequence\": None,\n                \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\": 0, \"output_tokens\": 0},\n            },\n        })\n\n    content_blocks = chunk.get(\"content\", [])\n    for block in content_blocks:\n        block_type = block.get(\"type\")\n        block_id = block.get(\"id\", \"0\")\n\n        if block_type == \"text\":\n            idx, is_new = state.get_or_create_index(block_id)\n            if is_new:\n                if state.current_block_id is not None and state.current_block_id != block_id:\n                    prev_idx = state.started_blocks.get(state.current_block_id, 0)\n                    events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": prev_idx})\n                state.current_block_id = block_id\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"content_block\": {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"\"},\n                })\n            text = block.get(\"text\", \"\")\n            if text:\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"delta\": {\"type\": \"text_delta\", \"text\": text},\n                })\n\n        elif block_type == \"tool_call\":\n            tool_id = block.get(\"id\", f\"toolu_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]}\")\n            idx, is_new = state.get_or_create_index(tool_id)\n            if is_new:\n                if state.current_block_id is not None and state.current_block_id != tool_id:\n                    prev_idx = state.started_blocks.get(state.current_block_id, 0)\n                    events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": prev_idx})\n                state.current_block_id = tool_id\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"content_block\": {\n                        \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                        \"id\": tool_id,\n                        \"name\": block.get(\"raw_name\", \"\"),\n                        \"input\": {},\n                    },\n                })\n            raw_args = block.get(\"raw_arguments\", \"\")\n            if raw_args:\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"delta\": {\n                        \"type\": \"input_json_delta\",\n                        \"partial_json\": raw_args,\n                    },\n                })\n\n        elif block_type == \"thought\":\n            idx, is_new = state.get_or_create_index(f\"thinking_{block_id}\")\n            if is_new:\n                if state.current_block_id is not None:\n                    prev_idx = state.started_blocks.get(state.current_block_id, 0)\n                    events.append({\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": prev_idx})\n                state.current_block_id = f\"thinking_{block_id}\"\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"content_block\": {\"type\": \"thinking\", \"thinking\": \"\"},\n                })\n            text = block.get(\"text\", \"\")\n            if text:\n                events.append({\n                    \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                    \"index\": idx,\n                    \"delta\": {\"type\": \"thinking_delta\", \"thinking\": text},\n                })\n\n    usage = chunk.get(\"usage\")\n    if usage:\n        state.model = chunk.get(\"variant_name\", state.model)\n\n    return events\n```\n\n### Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_llm_proxy_translate.py -v`\nExpected: all tests PASS\n\n### Step ---

## Report

### What was implemented

Created `backend/app/llm_proxy_translate.py` -- the module that translates between Anthropic Messages API format and TensorZero `/inference` format. This module contains three main components:

1. **`anthropic_to_tensorzero_request()`** -- Converts an Anthropic Messages API request body into TensorZero `/inference` format. Key translations:
   - Anthropic `"content": "text"` (plain string) and `"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "..."}]` (list) both convert to TensorZero `"content": [{"type": "text", "value": "..."}]`
   - Anthropic `"system"` field (string or list of blocks) becomes a system-role message prepended to the messages array
   - Tool use/result blocks are translated to TensorZero equivalents
   - Only safe, known fields (`max_tokens`, `temperature`, `top_p`, `tools`, `tool_choice`) are passed through to `params.chat_completion` -- Anthropic-specific fields like `thinking` and `metadata` are deliberately stripped

2. **`StreamState`** dataclass -- Tracks state during streaming response translation (block indices, which blocks have been started, message ID, etc.)

3. **`tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events()`** -- Converts TensorZero streaming chunks into Anthropic SSE event dicts (`message_start`, `content_block_start`, `content_block_delta`, `content_block_stop`, `message_delta`, `message_stop`). Handles text, tool_call, and thought block types.

### What was tested and test results

Created `backend/tests/test_llm_proxy_translate.py` with 11 tests covering both request translation and response streaming translation:

**Request translation tests (7):**
- `test_basic_text_message` -- plain string content converts correctly
- `test_system_prompt_added_as_system_message` -- system prompt becomes first message
- `test_content_list_messages` -- list-of-blocks content with `"text"` key maps to `"value"`
- `test_max_tokens_passed_through` -- max_tokens lands in `params.chat_completion`
- `test_tools_passed_through` -- tools array is forwarded
- `test_model_name_set_correctly` -- model_name is set from the tensorzero_model parameter
- `test_anthropic_specific_fields_stripped` -- `thinking`, `metadata` are not leaked into params

**Response streaming tests (4):**
- `test_text_chunk_to_anthropic_events` -- first text chunk produces `content_block_start` + `content_block_delta`
- `test_text_chunk_continuation` -- subsequent chunks for the same block produce only `content_block_delta`
- `test_tool_call_chunk_to_anthropic_events` -- tool_call chunks produce `content_block_start` with `type: tool_use`
- `test_done_produces_message_delta_and_stop` -- `finalize()` produces `content_block_stop`, `message_delta`, `message_stop`

**All 11 tests pass.**

### Files changed

- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/app/llm_proxy_translate.py` (199 lines)
- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/tests/test_llm_proxy_translate.py` (153 lines)

### Self-review findings

- The implementation faithfully handles the key format differences between Anthropic and TensorZero: `"text"` vs `"value"` key naming, string vs list content, and the SSE event protocol differences.
- The `StreamState` properly tracks block indices to avoid emitting duplicate `content_block_start` events for continuation chunks.
- Anthropic-specific fields (`thinking`, `metadata`) are excluded by using an allowlist approach -- only known safe fields are forwarded to TensorZero params, rather than trying to blocklist specific fields.
- The `thought` block type handler in the response translator provides forward-compatibility for extended thinking responses, even though it is not exercised by current tests.

### Issues or concerns

None. The module is straightforward, well-tested, and ready for integration with the LLM proxy router (Task 7).

Validates session UUID tokens, translates requests\nto TensorZero /inference format, and translates streaming responses\nback to Anthropic SSE format.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse, JSONResponse\nimport httpx\n\nfrom app.config import TENSORZERO_BASE_URL\nfrom app.token_store import proxy_token_store\nfrom app.model_map import resolve_model\nfrom app.llm_proxy_translate import (\n    anthropic_to_tensorzero_request,\n    tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events,\n    StreamState,\n)\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/llm-proxy\")\n\n# Read-only discovery endpoints (no auth required)\n_UNAUTHENTICATED_PATHS = {\"v1/models\"}\n\n\ndef _extract_session_token(request: Request) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract session token from x-api-key header or Authorization: Bearer.\"\"\"\n    return (\n        request.headers.get(\"x-api-key\")\n        or request.headers.get(\"authorization\", \"\")[len(\"Bearer \"):]\n        or None\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/v1/models\")\nasync def list_models():\n    \"\"\"Return available models for SDK discovery.\n\n    The Claude Agent SDK calls GET /v1/models during startup to resolve\n    short model aliases. We return a static list based on the model map.\n    \"\"\"\n    from app.model_map import _DEFAULT_MAP\n\n    models = []\n    seen = set()\n    for sdk_name in _DEFAULT_MAP:\n        if sdk_name not in seen:\n            models.append({\n                \"id\": sdk_name,\n                \"object\": \"model\",\n                \"created\": 0,\n                \"owned_by\": \"tensorzero\",\n            })\n            seen.add(sdk_name)\n\n    return {\"object\": \"list\", \"data\": models}\n\n\n@router.post(\"/v1/messages\")\nasync def proxy_messages(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Handle POST /v1/messages — the main Anthropic Messages API endpoint.\"\"\"\n    session_token=[REDACTED](request)\n    if not session_token or not proxy_token_store.validate_token(session_token):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail=\"Invalid or expired session token\")\n\n    body = await request.json()\n    model_name = body.get(\"model\", \"claude-sonnet-4-6\")\n    tensorzero_model = resolve_model(model_name)\n    is_streaming = body.get(\"stream\", False)\n\n    tz_request = anthropic_to_tensorzero_request(body, tensorzero_model)\n\n    if is_streaming:\n        return StreamingResponse(\n            _stream_response(tz_request, model_name),\n            media_type=\"text/event-stream\",\n            headers={\n                \"Cache-Control\": \"no-cache\",\n                \"Connection\": \"keep-alive\",\n                \"X-Accel-Buffering\": \"no\",\n            },\n        )\n    else:\n        return await _non_streaming_response(tz_request, model_name)\n\n\nasync def _stream_response(tz_request: dict, model_name: str):\n    \"\"\"Stream TensorZero response as Anthropic-format SSE events.\"\"\"\n    state = StreamState(model=model_name)\n\n    client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0))\n    try:\n        async with client.stream(\n            \"POST\",\n            f\"{TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}/inference\",\n            json=tz_request,\n        ) as response:\n            if response.status_code != 200:\n                error_body = await response.aread()\n                error_text = error_body.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n                logger.error(\n                    \"TensorZero returned %d: %s\", response.status_code, error_text\n                )\n                yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'error': {'type': 'api_error', 'message': error_text}})}\\n\\n\"\n                return\n\n            async for line in response.aiter_lines():\n                if not line.startswith(\"data: \"):\n                    continue\n                raw = line[6:].strip()\n                if raw == \"[DONE]\":\n                    break\n                try:\n                    chunk = json.loads(raw)\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                    continue\n\n                events = tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events(chunk, state)\n                for event in events:\n                    event_name = event[\"type\"]\n                    yield f\"event: {event_name}\\ndata: {json.dumps(event)}\\n\\n\"\n\n        # Finalize\n        usage = {\"output_tokens\": 0}\n        final_events = state.finalize(usage=usage)\n        for event in final_events:\n            event_name = event[\"type\"]\n            yield f\"event: {event_name}\\ndata: {json.dumps(event)}\\n\\n\"\n\n    except httpx.ConnectError:\n        logger.error(\"Cannot connect to TensorZero at %s\", TENSORZERO_BASE_URL)\n        yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'error': {'type': 'api_error', 'message': f'LLM gateway unreachable at {TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}'}})}\\n\\n\"\n    except Exception:\n        logger.exception(\"Error streaming from TensorZero\")\n        yield f\"event: error\\ndata: {json.dumps({'type': 'error', 'error': {'type': 'api_error', 'message': 'Internal proxy error'}})}\\n\\n\"\n    finally:\n        await client.aclose()\n\n\nasync def _non_streaming_response(tz_request: dict, model_name: str) -> JSONResponse:\n    \"\"\"Handle non-streaming inference and return Anthropic-format JSON.\"\"\"\n    tz_request[\"stream\"] = False\n\n    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0)) as client:\n        try:\n            resp = await client.post(\n                f\"{TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}/inference\",\n                json=tz_request,\n            )\n        except httpx.ConnectError:\n            raise HTTPException(\n                status_code=502,\n                detail=f\"LLM gateway unreachable at {TENSORZERO_BASE_URL}\",\n            )\n\n        if resp.status_code != 200:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=resp.status_code, detail=resp.text)\n\n        tz_data = resp.json()\n\n    content = []\n    for block in tz_data.get(\"content\", []):\n        if block.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n            content.append({\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": block.get(\"text\", \"\")})\n        elif block.get(\"type\") == \"tool_call\":\n            arguments = block.get(\"raw_arguments\", \"{}\")\n            try:\n                parsed_args = json.loads(arguments)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                parsed_args = {}\n            content.append({\n                \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                \"id\": block.get(\"id\", \"\"),\n                \"name\": block.get(\"raw_name\", block.get(\"name\", \"\")),\n                \"input\": parsed_args,\n            })\n\n    usage = tz_data.get(\"usage\", {})\n\n    return JSONResponse({\n        \"id\": tz_data.get(\"inference_id\", \"\"),\n        \"type\": \"message\",\n        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n        \"content\": content,\n        \"model\": model_name,\n        \"stop_reason\": \"end_turn\",\n        \"stop_sequence\": None,\n        \"usage\": {\n            \"input_tokens\": usage.get(\"input_tokens\", 0),\n            \"output_tokens\": usage.get(\"output_tokens\", 0),\n        },\n    })\n\n\n@router.api_route(\"/{path:path}\", methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\", \"PATCH\"])\nasync def catch_all(path: str, request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Catch-all for unhandled paths — return 404.\"\"\"\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\"Unknown path: /llm-proxy/{path}\")\n```\n\n### Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_llm_proxy.py -v`\nExpected: all 5 tests PASS\n\n### Step 5: Commit\n\n```bash\ngit add backend/app/llm_proxy.py backend/tests/test_llm_proxy.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(llm-proxy): add LLM proxy router with auth and TensorZero forwarding\"\n```\n\n## Context\n\nTask 7 of 10. This is the main router that replaces `proxy.py`. It uses:\n- `token_store.py` (Task 4) for UUID token validation\n- `model_map.py` (Task 5) for model name resolution\n- `llm_proxy_translate.py` (Task 6) for request/response format translation\n- `config.py` for `TENSORZERO_BASE_URL`\n\nThe existing `proxy.py` has prefix `/anthropic`. The new router uses prefix `/llm-proxy`.\n\n## Your Job\n\n1. Follow TDD: write tests first, verify they fail, implement, verify they pass\n2. Commit your work\n\nWork from: /Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy\n\n## Report Format\n\nWhen done, report:\n- What you implemented\n- What you tested and test results\n- Files changed\n- Self-review findings (if any)\n- Any issues or concerns",

---

## Report

### What I implemented

Created `backend/app/llm_proxy.py` -- the FastAPI router that replaces `proxy.py`. The router:

1. **Token authentication** via `_extract_session_token()` -- extracts tokens from either `x-api-key` header or `Authorization: Bearer` header, then validates against the `proxy_token_store`.

2. **`GET /llm-proxy/v1/models`** -- unauthenticated endpoint for SDK discovery. Returns the static model list from `model_map._DEFAULT_MAP` so the Claude Agent SDK can resolve model aliases at startup.

3. **`POST /llm-proxy/v1/messages`** -- the main Anthropic Messages API endpoint. Requires authentication, then:
   - Resolves the model name via `resolve_model()`
   - Translates the request body via `anthropic_to_tensorzero_request()`
   - For streaming requests: returns a `StreamingResponse` that forwards TensorZero chunks as Anthropic SSE events using `tensorzero_chunk_to_anthropic_events()` and `StreamState.finalize()`
   - For non-streaming requests: makes a single POST to TensorZero and translates the response back to Anthropic JSON format

4. **Catch-all route** -- returns 404 for any unrecognized paths under `/llm-proxy/`.

### What I tested and test results

Created `backend/tests/test_llm_proxy.py` with 5 tests:

| Test | Description | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `test_rejects_missing_auth` | POST /v1/messages with no auth header returns 401 | PASSED |
| `test_rejects_invalid_x_api_key` | POST with invalid x-api-key returns 401 | PASSED |
| `test_rejects_invalid_bearer_token` | POST with invalid Bearer token returns 401 | PASSED |
| `test_rejects_revoked_token` | POST with a revoked token returns 401 | PASSED |
| `test_models_endpoint_no_auth_required` | GET /v1/models returns 200 with model list, no auth needed | PASSED |

TDD process followed: tests were written first, verified to fail with `ModuleNotFoundError`, then the implementation was created and all 5 tests passed.

### Files changed

- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/app/llm_proxy.py` (201 lines) -- the LLM proxy router
- **Created:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/feature/llm-proxy/backend/tests/test_llm_proxy.py` (68 lines) -- the test suite

### Self-review findings

1. **Token extraction logic** -- The `_extract_session_token()` function properly handles three cases: (a) `x-api-key` header present, (b) `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header present, (c) neither present. The implementation avoids the edge case in the task description's version where an empty string after `Bearer ` slicing could evaluate to falsy. I used explicit checks with `startswith("Bearer ")` and a length check.

2. **Error handling in streaming** -- The stream response properly handles TensorZero connection errors (`httpx.ConnectError`), non-200 responses from TensorZero, and general exceptions, emitting Anthropic-format error SSE events in each case. The `httpx.AsyncClient` is always closed in the `finally` block.

3. **Non-streaming response** -- Correctly translates both text blocks and tool_call blocks from TensorZero format back to Anthropic format (text -> text, tool_call -> tool_use with JSON-parsed arguments).

### Issues or concerns

None. The implementation cleanly uses all the dependencies from earlier tasks (token_store, model_map, llm_proxy_translate, config) and follows the established patterns. The router is ready to be wired into `main.py` in Task 8.
